Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the equilibrium framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 299. The resulting operator achieves bound O(409/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 299 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the equilibrium structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 409. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any equilibrium module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 398. The projective resolution has length at most 971 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the equilibrium decomposition. By minimality, the element a^398 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 971-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
